## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 1988年5月25日。山形県の戸沢村（とざわむら）。
【制作メモ】ASSET-001 [Lovart動画] 冒頭は必ず動画（恒久ルール）
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 夜明け前の戸沢村を最上川ぞいに進む空撮。この動画の上に日付と地名のテロップを重ねる
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
High aerial still view at first light in late May over a small mountain village in Yamagata, Japan: a wide river winding west through a deep green valley, dark forested ridges pressing in from both sides, a thin line of dark-roofed houses along one road on the near bank, fresh early-summer foliage on the slopes, thin white mist lying on the water. Late May, cold blue pre-dawn tones, dim but NOT pure black — the river, the ridgelines and the rooftops all stay clearly readable. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. Ominous quiet before something begins. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
Slow forward aerial drone movement at first light in late May over a narrow Japanese mountain valley in Yamagata, Japan: a wide river winding west below, dark forested ridges in fresh early-summer green, a thin line of village rooftops along one road, white mist drifting off the water, steady ominous glide. Cold blue pre-dawn light, dim but not pure black. No snow anywhere. No people visible. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 動画の上に「1988年5月25日」を白テキストでフェードイン（1秒）、続けて下段に「山形県 戸沢村」。低いドローン音のBGMを開始。

---


## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 深夜、タケノコ採りに行った男性が遺体で発見。
【制作メモ】ASSET-002 [Lovart動画] 冒頭フックは実写風動画
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 深夜の沢すじを、いくつもの懐中電灯の光が下りていく。人物は光だけで示す
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A steep wooded ravine in a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan at night in late May, seen from above the stream bed: fresh green bamboo grass and young beech leaves crowding both banks, wet dark rock in the stream below, and a scatter of torch beams cutting down through the trees from several directions at once, each beam catching drifting insects and leaf edges. Late May night, dim but NOT pure black — the ravine walls, the stream and every torch beam stay clearly readable. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. Frantic silent searching. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no humans visible, only the torch beams. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A handheld night shot looking down into a steep wooded ravine in a Japanese mountain village in late May. Several torch beams sweep and jolt down through the fresh green undergrowth toward the stream bed, crossing each other, catching drifting insects. One beam suddenly stops moving and holds still on the dark ground below. Dim but not pure black. No snow. No people visible, only the moving beams. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 光が一本止まったところでカット。「深夜」テロップを左上に。衝撃SEは入れず、無音に近い環境音のまま次へ。

---


## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: さらに、200m先でクルミ拾いに出た女性も遺体で見つかり、
【制作メモ】ASSET-003 [Lovart動画] 冒頭フックは実写風動画
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 秋の山裾に立つクルミの大木を、朝の光の中で見上げる。根元の草が広く踏み倒されている
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
Looking up at a huge old walnut tree rising at the foot of a forested slope behind a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan in late October, its branches heavy with yellowing leaves against a cold pale sky, fallen walnuts scattered across the ground at its base, and a wide patch of grass beneath it pressed flat and dusty and rumpled. Autumn foliage in gold and rust on the slope behind, dry brown grass, fallen leaves. Late October morning, cold flat overcast light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. Quiet and wrong. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A slow steady tilt down the trunk of a huge old walnut tree at the foot of a forested slope in a Japanese mountain village in late October, from the yellowing branches against a pale cold sky down to the fallen walnuts and the wide flattened patch of grass at its base. Autumn gold and rust on the slope behind, no snow. Almost no wind, one leaf falling. No people visible. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style. No blood, no wounds, no gore.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「200m先」テロップ。1件目からの距離であることを矢印つきの小さな図で右下に添える。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: そこから5km先にいた女性は家族5人でクリ拾い中に、
【制作メモ】ASSET-004 [Lovart動画] 冒頭フックは実写風動画
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 秋の持ち山で、離れ離れにクリを拾う家族5人を引きの構図で。誰もが互いを見ていない
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A wide high view of a family's own chestnut grove on a gentle forested slope in Yamagata, Japan in late October: five Japanese adults in ordinary country work clothes scattered far apart among the trees, each bent over the leaf-covered ground gathering chestnuts alone, none of them looking at any of the others, separated by twenty or thirty metres of trees. Left to right: 1) a woman in her 60s in a red-checked flannel shirt and beige apron; 2) a lean man in his 60s in a navy work jacket and grey cap; 3) a man in his 30s in a brown blouson; 4) a woman in her 30s in a mustard cardigan; 5) a teenage boy in a grey sweatshirt. Every person is a distinct individual and no two dressed alike. NO TWO OF THEM SHARE A FACE, A HAIRSTYLE OR AN OUTFIT, and no two of them are the same height. Autumn gold and rust in the canopy, dry fallen leaves and chestnut husks on the ground. Late October midday, soft overcast light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A slow drifting wide shot through a family chestnut grove on a forested slope in Yamagata, Japan in late October. Five Japanese adults in ordinary country work clothes are scattered far apart among the trees, each gathering chestnuts alone and none of them looking at the others. The camera glides gently sideways behind the trunks so the figures pass in and out of view. Autumn gold and rust, dry leaves, no snow. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「5km先」テロップ。人と人の間に距離の目盛りを一瞬だけ薄く重ねる。

---


## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: クマに襲われ帰らぬ人に、、
【制作メモ】ASSET-005 [Lovart動画] フック最大の画（HOOK-IMAGE）
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 秋の斜面に、拾いかけのクリの実と籠が転がっている。その奥の暗い林の中へ、ツキノワグマが四足で走り去っていく
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A wide low view of the leaf-covered floor of a chestnut grove on a forested slope in Yamagata, Japan in late October. In the foreground a woven basket lies tipped over on its side with chestnuts spilled in a wide arc across the dry leaves, a pair of white cotton work gloves dropped apart from each other, and the leaf litter pressed flat and dusty and rumpled in a long streak leading away up the slope. In the middle distance an adult Japanese black bear (Asiatic black bear, Ursus thibetanus japonicus), 140cm body length and 84kg, glossy jet-black coat with a white crescent-moon patch across the chest, large round ears, short blunt muzzle, NO shoulder hump, is frozen mid-run ON ALL FOURS away from the camera toward the dark mouth of the forest, dry leaves suspended in the air behind its hind legs, its shoulder no higher than an adult's waist and clearly much smaller than a grizzly or a brown bear. NOT reared up, NOT on two legs. Autumn gold and rust in the canopy, cold flat overcast light. The mouth of the forest is dim but NOT pure black — the bear's black shape stays clearly readable against the trunks. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A locked-off wide low shot of the leaf-covered floor of a chestnut grove in Yamagata, Japan in late October. A tipped-over basket and spilled chestnuts fill the foreground, the leaf litter pressed flat in a long streak leading up the slope. In the middle distance an adult Japanese black bear, 140cm long, glossy jet-black with a white crescent chest patch, large round ears and no shoulder hump, runs on all fours away from the camera into the mouth of the forest and disappears, kicking up dry leaves. The forest shade is dim but not pure black and the bear stays clearly readable until it is gone. It never stands upright and never looks back. Only the bear moves. Cold overcast autumn light, no snow. No people visible. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 音を一瞬すべて消してこのカットを見せる（3〜4秒）。クマが林に消えた直後に心拍音を立ち上げる。「帰らぬ人に」のテロップは出さず、ナレーションだけで受ける。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: なぜ、立て続けに同じ村で3度も悲劇が繰り返されたのか。
【制作メモ】ASSET-006 [Lovart動画] 冒頭フックは実写風動画
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 村を横切る川と、その両岸の山を高い空撮で。問いのテキストは編集者が重ねる
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A high aerial still view over a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan in late October: a wide river cutting west through the middle of the frame, forested ridges in autumn gold and rust rising steeply on both banks, small clusters of dark-roofed houses and harvested fields squeezed onto the narrow flat land beside the water. The upper third of the frame is deliberately left as clean open uncluttered sky for a large caption added in editing. Cold flat overcast afternoon light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. Vast, quiet and closed in. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no humans visible. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A very slow high aerial drift over a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan in late October: a wide river cutting west through the frame, forested ridges in autumn gold and rust on both banks, small clusters of dark rooftops and harvested fields on the narrow flat land beside the water. The camera eases forward with funeral calm, clean open sky held across the top of the frame. No snow. No people visible. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 上部の余白に「なぜ、同じ村で3度も繰り返されたのか」を白文字でゆっくりフェードイン。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: そしてなぜ、このクマは満腹なのに人を食べたのか？
【制作メモ】ASSET-007 [Lovart動画] 冒頭フックは実写風動画
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: クリの実が一面に散った林床のクローズアップから、その上に立つツキノワグマの前足へ寄る
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A low close view of a chestnut grove floor in Yamagata, Japan in late October, thick with fallen chestnuts still in their split spiky husks and dry brown leaves, food lying everywhere within reach. At the top of the frame, close and looming, are the front legs, chest and lowered head of an adult Japanese black bear (Asiatic black bear, Ursus thibetanus japonicus), 140cm body length and 84kg, glossy jet-black coat, a white crescent-moon patch across the chest, large round ears, short blunt muzzle, NO shoulder hump, poised ON ALL FOURS directly over the scattered nuts without eating them, its claws spread on the leaf litter. NOT reared up, NOT on two legs. Its shoulder is no higher than an adult's waist and it is clearly much smaller than a grizzly or a brown bear. Cold flat overcast light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera, 50mm lens. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A slow push in low across a chestnut grove floor in Yamagata, Japan in late October, thick with fallen chestnuts in split husks and dry leaves. The front legs and lowered head of an adult Japanese black bear, 140cm long, glossy jet-black with a white crescent chest patch, large round ears and no shoulder hump, stand on all fours over the nuts without eating them. The head lifts slowly and turns away from the food toward the camera. It never stands upright. Cold overcast autumn light, no snow. No people visible. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「満腹なのに、なぜ人を食べたのか？」を下段に白テロップ。クマが顔を上げるところで音を切る。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 地形図とともに解説します。
【制作メモ】ASSET-008 [Google Earth]
- 【Google Earth】
シーン: 日本列島から山形県、そして戸沢村へ2段階でズームインして「地形図で解説する」宣言に応える
検索座標: 38.7376, 140.1436（戸沢村役場）
カメラ高度: 高度300km（東北全体）から高度10kmまでズームイン
カメラ角度: 真上から斜め45°へ、3D地形ON
向き: 南東から北西を見下ろす（最上川が左右に横切る向き）
地点A（戸沢村役場）: 38.7376, 140.1436
→ 編集者指示: ズーム終点で赤ピンと「戸沢村」ラベルを役場位置に表示。ナレーションに合わせて2段階ズーム。

---


## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 戸沢村は、山形県の北にあり、人口およそ4,300人。
【制作メモ】ASSET-009 [Google Earth]
- 【Google Earth】
シーン: 山形県全体を俯瞰し、県の北部にある戸沢村の位置と、村を横切る最上川を示す
検索座標: 38.7376, 140.1436（戸沢村役場）を画面上寄りに置き、山形県全体が入る構図
カメラ高度: 120,000〜150,000m
カメラ角度: 斜め30°、3D地形ON
向き: 南から北を見下ろす
地点A（戸沢村役場）: 38.7376, 140.1436
→ 編集者指示: 山形県の輪郭を白線でなぞり、戸沢村に赤ピンと「戸沢村」ラベル。「人口 約4,300人」を左下にテロップ。最上川の流路を細い水色のラインでなぞる。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 自然に囲まれた村で、定期的にクマの目撃情報が相次いでいました。
【制作メモ】ASSET-010 [Lovart動画]
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 家並みのすぐ裏まで迫る杉林の際を、ツキノワグマが一頭、四足で横切っていく
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A view from a village lane in a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan in late May, looking past the corner of a dark-tiled farmhouse toward a steep cedar plantation that rises directly behind the houses, only a few metres of vegetable patch between the back wall and the tree line. An adult Japanese black bear (Asiatic black bear, Ursus thibetanus japonicus), 140cm body length and 84kg, glossy jet-black coat, a white crescent-moon patch across the chest, large round ears, short blunt muzzle, NO shoulder hump, is frozen mid-stride while walking calmly ON ALL FOURS across the strip of grass at the forest edge, its shoulder no higher than an adult's waist and clearly much smaller than a grizzly or a brown bear. NOT reared up, NOT on two legs. Fresh early-summer green on the slope, late May morning, soft flat light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. Ordinary and unremarkable, which is the point. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera, 85mm lens. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A locked-off shot from a village lane in a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan in late May, looking past a dark-tiled farmhouse toward the cedar plantation that rises right behind it. An adult Japanese black bear, 140cm long, glossy jet-black with a white crescent chest patch, large round ears and no shoulder hump, is walking calmly on all fours across the strip of grass at the forest edge from right to left and is gone into the trees. It never stands upright and never looks at the camera. Fresh early-summer green, no snow. No people visible. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「クマの目撃情報が相次いでいた」テロップ。BGMは付けず、鳥の声と風だけ。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: この村に住む61歳の男性が、タケノコを採りに山へ入ります。
【制作メモ】ASSET-012 [キャラアニメーション]
- 【キャラアニメーション】
シーン: CHAR-01が背負い籠を背負い、山道の入り口へ向かって歩き出す
キャラプロンプト（1:1）:
```
(CHAR-01 再利用) Cute cartoon character design, thick black outlines, flat cel-shaded colors, large expressive eyes, slightly chibi proportions, children's animation style. CHAR-01: Japanese farmer, 61 years old, faded dark green work jacket, brown work trousers, black rubber boots, beige towel around his neck, woven bamboo basket on his back, CLEAN-SHAVEN with no beard, no moustache and no stubble, walking forward with an easy practised stride, one hand steadying the basket strap, calm everyday expression. He has a large head, a short compact torso and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw him with realistic adult proportions. Full body. White background. 1:1 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
背景プロンプト（16:9）:
```
The entrance of a narrow mountain footpath at the edge of a Japanese village in Yamagata, Japan in late May: a gap in a wall of fresh green bamboo grass, young beech and cedar closing over the path a few metres in, wet dark soil underfoot, a stone marker worn smooth beside the path. Fresh early-summer foliage, late May morning, soft flat light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. The middle of the frame is left as clear open space. Framed from adult eye height roughly 1.5 metres above the ground and about four metres back, the camera angled only slightly downward: the ground occupies just the lower third of the frame. Not a ground-level shot, not a close-up. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「61歳・農業」のテロップをCHAR-01の下に。CHAR-01を画面左から山道の入り口へゆっくりスライド（3秒）。穏やかな環境音のみ。

---


## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 家族にタケノコを採りにいくと伝え、午前10時ごろに出発。
【制作メモ】ASSET-013 [キャラアニメーション]
- 【キャラアニメーション】
シーン: 玄関先でCHAR-01がCHAR-02に一言かけて出ていく。ごく普通の朝のやりとり
キャラプロンプト（1:1）:
```
(CHAR-01 再利用)(CHAR-02 再利用) Cute cartoon character design, thick black outlines, flat cel-shaded colors, large expressive eyes, slightly chibi proportions, children's animation style. Left to right: 1) CHAR-01: Japanese farmer, 61 years old, faded dark green work jacket, brown work trousers, black rubber boots, beige towel at his neck, woven basket on his back, half-turned back over his shoulder with one hand raised in a small casual wave, mouth open in an easy word; 2) CHAR-02: Japanese woman in her early 60s, dusty-rose work smock over dark navy monpe trousers and a grey cardigan, grey hair in a low bun, standing with a dish cloth in both hands, nodding with a gentle everyday smile. Every person is a distinct individual and no two dressed alike. NO TWO OF THEM SHARE A FACE, A HAIRSTYLE OR AN OUTFIT. Both have large heads, short compact torsos and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw them with realistic adult proportions. Full body. White background. 1:1 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
背景プロンプト（16:9）:
```
The front entrance of an old Japanese country house in Yamagata, Japan in late May: a sliding wooden door pushed half open, worn stepping stones across a small swept yard, a pair of rubber boots by the step, a bucket and a bundle of bamboo poles against the wall, fresh green mountains rising close behind the roof. Late May morning, warm soft light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. The middle of the frame is left as clear open space. Framed from adult eye height roughly 1.5 metres above the ground and about four metres back. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「午前10時ごろ」テロップを左上に。日常の音（鶏の声、遠くの軽トラ）だけ。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 奥さんは夕方には戻ってくるだろうと思い、そのまま見送ることに。

- 【キャラアニメーション】

(CHAR-02 再利用) Cute cartoon character design, thick black outlines, flat cel-shaded colors, large expressive eyes, slightly chibi proportions, children's animation style. CHAR-02: Japanese woman in her early 60s, dusty-rose work smock over dark navy monpe trousers and a grey cardigan, grey hair in a low bun, standing at ease with one hand shading her eyes, looking off to one side, earnest and level expression, eyebrows relaxed with no furrow between them, eyes open at their normal width, mouth closed in a soft even line. NOT grinning, NOT angry, NOT frightened, NOT grim. She has a large head, a short compact torso and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw her with realistic adult proportions. Full body. White background. 1:1 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
背景プロンプト（16:9）:

A view from the gateway of an old Japanese farmhouse in Yamagata, Japan in late May, looking up a narrow lane that curves away between vegetable plots toward the fresh green forested slope behind the village. Bamboo poles leaning on a fence, a persimmon tree just coming into leaf, morning light along the lane. Late May, soft warm flat light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. The middle of the frame is left as clear open space. Framed from adult eye height roughly 1.5 metres above the ground and about four metres back. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.
```

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: しかし、これ以降、男性は二度と家族のもとへは戻ってきませんでした。
【制作メモ】ASSET-015 [Lovart動画]
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 誰も歩いてこない山道を、夕方の光がゆっくり降りていく。道の先は暗い林

An adult male Japanese black bear (Asiatic black bear, Ursus thibetanus japonicus), 140cm body length and 84kg, caught full in a torch beam on the far bank of a mountain stream in Yamagata, Japan at night in late May. Glossy jet-black coat, a white crescent-moon patch across the chest, large round ears, a short blunt muzzle, NO shoulder hump, a stocky but slender frame. It stands ON ALL FOURS with its chest and both shoulders squared to the camera, head dropped low and driven forward between bunched shoulders, jaws wrenched wide open in a savage snarl with the long curved canine fangs and the whole row of teeth completely bared, black lips peeled back off pink gums, muzzle creased into deep folds, ears pinned flat against the skull, the fur along its neck, shoulders and spine bristled up into a raised ridge, claws fully extended and hooked into the wet gravel, small eyes narrowed to slits and fixed straight on the lens with the whites showing at their edges. Ferocious, enraged, single-minded predatory fury. Its shoulder is no higher than an adult's waist and it is clearly much smaller than a grizzly or a brown bear. NOT reared up, NOT on two legs. Fresh green bamboo grass behind it, late May night, dim but NOT pure black — the bear stays clearly readable in and around the beam. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera, 85mm lens. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```

A torch beam swings across the far bank of a mountain stream in Yamagata, Japan at night in late May and lands full on an adult Japanese black bear, 140cm long, glossy jet-black with a white crescent chest patch, large round ears, a short blunt muzzle and no shoulder hump. It is squared to the camera on all fours, head low between bunched shoulders, jaws wide open in a snarl with every tooth bared, ears flat, the fur along its neck and spine raised into a ridge, eyes locked on the lens. It does not move away. The beam shakes. Dim but not pure black. No snow. No people visible. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 消防団員、警察官、猟友会員。あわせて57人が、四つの班に分かれて山へ入ります。
【制作メモ】ASSET-066 [キャラアニメーション]
- 【キャラアニメーション】
シーン: 夜明けの広場で、班ごとに分かれて整列する捜索隊
キャラプロンプト（1:1）:
```
[Generic group](CHAR-06 再利用) Cute cartoon character design, thick black outlines, flat cel-shaded colors, large expressive eyes, slightly chibi proportions, children's animation style. Left to right: 1) CHAR-06: Japanese man, 26 years old, navy volunteer fire brigade jacket with plain reflective bands and no lettering, dark grey trousers, black boots, white cotton gloves, standing at attention; 2) a Japanese police officer in his 40s in a plain dark blue uniform with no crest, no emblem and no lettering, holding a folded paper; 3) a Japanese hunter in his 60s in an olive-brown hunting jacket and orange cap with a plain bolt-action rifle slung muzzle-down on his shoulder; 4) a Japanese man in his 50s in a dark green fire brigade jacket with a whistle at his collar; 5) a Japanese man in his 30s in a khaki work jacket and towel headband. Every person is a distinct individual and no two dressed alike. NO TWO OF THEM SHARE A FACE, A HAIRSTYLE OR AN OUTFIT, and no two of them are the same height. All have large heads, short compact torsos and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw them with realistic adult proportions. Only their eye direction and head tilt may differ. Full body. White background. 1:1 aspect ratio. No lettering, no readable characters anywhere. Generate 1 image.
```
背景プロンプト（16:9）:
```
An open gravel yard beside a small community hall in a Japanese mountain village in Yamagata, Japan at first light in late October: a plain single-storey building with a corrugated roof, a bare flagpole, parked kei trucks at the edge, forested ridges in autumn gold and rust behind, thin mist over the fields. No real institution name, no crest, no emblem, no logo, no lettering, no readable characters anywhere. Late October dawn, cool light but NOT pure black — the yard, the building and the ridges all stay clearly readable. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「総勢57人・4班」テロップ。キャラを4つのかたまりに配置し、班ごとに枠で囲む。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: クマがまだいるかもしれないので、猟友会が銃を持って先導します。
【制作メモ】ASSET-067 [Lovart動画]
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 猟銃を構えたハンターの背中越しに、朝の暗い雑木林へ入っていく主観
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A shot from just behind and slightly above the shoulder of a Japanese hunter in an olive-brown hunting jacket and orange cap moving into a wood at the foot of a slope in Yamagata, Japan at first light in late October, a plain bolt-action hunting rifle held low across his body with the whole length of the rifle visible across the frame and the muzzle pointing forward into the trees. Only his back, shoulder, cap and hands are in frame and his face is not visible. Bare-branched walnut and chestnut trees, thick dry leaf litter, autumn gold and rust, thin mist between the trunks. Late October dawn, cool light, dim between the trunks but NOT pure black — the rifle, the hunter's shoulder and the trees all stay clearly readable. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera, 35mm lens. Documentary drama style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A slow handheld shot from just behind the shoulder of a Japanese hunter in an olive-brown jacket and orange cap walking into a wood at first light in late October in Yamagata, Japan, a plain bolt-action rifle held low across his body, muzzle forward. Only his back and shoulder are in frame, his face is never visible. Mist drifts between the bare trunks, dry leaves crunch underfoot, the camera sways with his steps. Dim but not pure black. No snow. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary drama style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 足音と落ち葉の音だけ。BGMは入れない。「猟友会が先導」テロップを小さく。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 女性が見つかったのは、5月の現場から200メートルの距離で、非常に近い場所。
【制作メモ】ASSET-071 [Google Earth]
- 【Google Earth】
シーン: 5月の現場と10月の現場が200メートルしか離れていないことを、同じ画面の中で示す
検索座標: 38.7938, 140.1434（戸沢村 神田地区・大字の代表点）
カメラ高度: 800〜1,200m
カメラ角度: 斜め45°、3D地形ON
向き: 南から北を見下ろす（杉沢集落が画面中央）
地点A（神田地区）: 約 38.7938, 140.1434
→ 編集者指示: 集落を中心に半径200mの白い円を描き「約200m」と表示。2つの現場の正確な位置は非公表のため、円の中に2つの目印だけを置き、実座標のピンは打たない。

A low aerial still view over a tiny hamlet in a Japanese mountain valley in Yamagata, Japan in late October: fifteen or so dark-tiled houses strung along one narrow road, small harvested fields and drying racks between them, and steep forested slopes in autumn gold and rust closing in from both sides so that the houses sit in a narrow band with the forest only metres from every back wall. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no humans visible. Generate 1 image.
```

```
A slow low aerial orbit around a tiny hamlet in a Japanese mountain valley in Yamagata, Japan in late October: fifteen or so dark-tiled houses along one narrow road, harvested fields between them, steep forested slopes in autumn gold and rust closing in from both sides only metres from the back walls. Thin mist drifts in the side valleys. Cool flat light, no snow. No people visible. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.

ナレーター: 実際、2016年の十和利山（とわりやま）でも、クマは移動して被害が広がっています。
【制作メモ】ASSET-100 [Google Earth]
- 【Google Earth】
シーン: 青森・秋田県境の十和利山を俯瞰し、被害が広がった範囲を示す
検索座標: 40.4269, 140.9851（十和利山 山頂・標高990.9m）
カメラ高度: 20,000〜30,000m
カメラ角度: 斜め35°、3D地形ON
向き: 南から北を見下ろす（十和田湖が奥に入る向き）
地点A（十和利山 山頂）: 40.4269, 140.9851（標高990.9m）
→ 編集者指示: 山頂に赤ピンと「十和利山 標高991m」ラベル。山麓側に「2016年」のテロップ。被害が複数地点に広がったことを、山の周囲に置いた複数の目印で示す。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: そして、戸沢村クマ事件の被害はさらに拡大することとなります。

シーン: 尾根を越えた先の谷。まだ何も起きていない秋の山が広がっている
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
A wide view over a second forested valley in Yamagata, Japan in late October seen from a ridge: layered slopes in autumn gold and rust receding into haze, a thin river far below, a few dark-tiled rooftops on the valley floor, thin mist in the side valleys, a heavy overcast sky pressing down on the ridgeline. The upper third of the frame is deliberately left as clean open uncluttered space for a large caption added in editing. Late October afternoon, cool flat light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. Nothing has happened here yet. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no humans visible. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A very slow aerial drift forward over a second forested valley in Yamagata, Japan in late October, seen from a ridge: layered slopes in autumn gold and rust receding into haze, a thin river far below, a few dark rooftops on the valley floor, mist drifting in the side valleys under a heavy overcast sky. Nothing moves but the mist. Cool flat light, no snow. No people visible. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```
→ 編集者指示: 上部の余白に「被害はさらに拡大する」を白文字でゆっくり出す。BGMを重く落として次の章へ渡す。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 家族5人が山へ向かいました。
【制作メモ】ASSET-104 [キャラアニメーション]
- 【キャラアニメーション】
シーン: 軽トラの荷台に籠を積み、家族5人が連れ立って歩き出す
キャラプロンプト（1:1）:
```
(CHAR-04 再利用)(CHAR-05 再利用)[Generic group] Cute cartoon character design, thick black outlines, flat cel-shaded colors, large expressive eyes, slightly chibi proportions, children's animation style. Left to right: 1) CHAR-04: Japanese woman, 61 years old, soft red-checked flannel shirt, beige field apron, dark brown trousers, grey trainers, white cotton work gloves, carrying a woven basket, smiling; 2) CHAR-05: Japanese man in his early 60s, navy zip-up work jacket, khaki trousers, black rubber boots, grey cap, carrying two empty sacks over one shoulder; 3) a Japanese man in his 30s in a brown blouson and jeans, carrying a long bamboo pole; 4) a Japanese woman in her 30s in a mustard cardigan and dark trousers, holding a thermos; 5) a Japanese teenage boy in a grey sweatshirt and trainers, hands in his pockets. Every person is a distinct individual and no two dressed alike. NO TWO OF THEM SHARE A FACE, A HAIRSTYLE OR AN OUTFIT, and no two of them are the same height. All have large heads, short compact torsos and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw them with realistic adult proportions. Full body. White background. 1:1 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
背景プロンプト（16:9）:
```
A narrow farm track at the foot of a forested slope in Yamagata, Japan in late October: a white kei mini truck parked at the edge with its tailgate down, harvested fields on one side, autumn gold and rust rising steeply on the other, thin morning mist over the valley. Late October morning, soft warm light. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. The middle of the frame is left as clear open space. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「家族5人」テロップ。5人を横一列に並べ、奥へゆっくりスライド。明るいBGM。

## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: それでも、クマはやってきたのです、、
【制作メモ】ASSET-111 [Lovart動画]
- 【Lovart動画】
シーン: 林の奥の暗がりから、ツキノワグマが四足で音もなく歩み出てくる
静止画プロンプト（16:9・フォトリアル）:
```
The shaded depth of a chestnut grove on a forested slope in Yamagata, Japan in late October, dry leaf litter filling the foreground and evenly spaced trunks receding into shade. An adult male Japanese black bear (Asiatic black bear, Ursus thibetanus japonicus), 140cm body length and 84kg, glossy jet-black coat with a white crescent-moon patch across the chest, large round ears, a short blunt muzzle and NO shoulder hump, is emerging from between the far trunks toward the camera ON ALL FOURS in a low creeping posture, head carried low, shoulders rolling, eyes fixed straight ahead, ears flattened right back against the skull, the fur along its neck and shoulders bristled up into a raised ridge, claws spread on the leaf litter. It is NOT calm, NOT curious and NOT gentle. Its shoulder is no higher than an adult's waist and it is clearly much smaller than a grizzly or a brown bear. NOT reared up, NOT on two legs. Autumn gold and rust overhead, late October midday, dim in the shade but NOT pure black — the bear's black shape stays clearly readable against the trunks. No snow anywhere, no frost, no ice, no winter. No people, no humans visible. No blood, no wounds, no gore. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera, 85mm lens. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. Generate 1 image.
```
Google Flow動画プロンプト:
```
A locked-off shot into the shaded depth of a chestnut grove in Yamagata, Japan in late October. An adult Japanese black bear, 140cm long, glossy jet-black with a white crescent chest patch, large round ears, a short blunt muzzle and no shoulder hump, slides out from between the far trunks and creeps toward the camera on all fours in a low stalking posture, placing each paw slowly and deliberately, ears pinned flat against the skull, black lips peeled back off the teeth in a low snarl, the fur along its neck and spine bristled up into a raised ridge, claws spread and hooked into the leaf litter. It never stands upright and never breaks its gaze. Dry leaves barely move. Dim but not pure black. No snow. No people visible. No blood, no gore. 5 seconds. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style.
```
→ 編集者指示: BGMを完全に止め、落ち葉を踏む音だけを立てる。クマが画面手前に達する直前でカットを切る。

All have large heads, short compact torsos and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw them with realistic adult proportions. Full body. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.

ナレーター: DNA鑑定をした結果、3人目の女性のものだと判明。
【制作メモ】ASSET-171 [キャラアニメーション]
- 【キャラアニメーション】
シーン: 白衣の検査員が、手元の結果を見て小さくうなずく
キャラプロンプト（1:1）:
```
[Generic group] Cute cartoon character design, thick black outlines, flat cel-shaded colors, large expressive eyes, slightly chibi proportions, children's animation style. A Japanese laboratory technician, woman in her 30s in a plain white lab coat with no crest, no emblem and no logo, hair tied back, seated posture holding a single blank sheet of paper in both hands at chest height and looking down at it, head dipping in one small nod, earnest and level expression, eyebrows relaxed with no furrow between them, eyes open at their normal width, mouth closed in a soft even line. NOT grinning, NOT angry, NOT frightened, NOT grim. Only the sheet of paper as a prop, nothing else around her. She has a large head, a short compact torso and short stubby arms and legs, roughly four to five heads tall. Do NOT draw her with realistic adult proportions. Full body. White background. 1:1 aspect ratio. No lettering, no readable characters anywhere. Generate 1 image.
```
背景プロンプト（16:9）:
```
A plain small laboratory bench in Japan at night in late October: a rack of empty glass test tubes, a microscope, a notebook with blank pages, a desk lamp, tiled wall behind. No real institution name, no crest, no emblem, no logo, no lettering, no readable characters anywhere. Late October night, cool even light, dim at the edges but NOT pure black — the bench and the equipment stay clearly readable. No snow anywhere, no frost, no winter. Photorealistic, shot on RED camera. Documentary style. 16:9 aspect ratio. No people, no figures, no humans visible. Empty landscape only. Generate 1 image.
```
→ 編集者指示: 「DNA鑑定」テロップ。1988年当時の技術ではないという指摘があるため、鑑定の年代は画面に出さない。

Autumn gold and rust behind, late October afternoon, dim near the ground but NOT pure black — the bear's black shape stays clearly readable against the leaf litter. An adult Japanese black bear, 140cm long, glossy jet-black with a white crescent chest patch, large round ears, a short blunt muzzle and no shoulder hump, drives straight toward the camera on all fours with its head carried low at knee height, claws fully extended and hooked into the leaves, ears pinned flat against the skull, black lips peeled back off the whole row of teeth in a savage snarl, the fur along its neck and shoulders bristled up into a raised ridge, dry leaves kicked up around its paws. ## 1. 全素材リスト（台本順）
ナレーター: 1977年から2020年までの山形県でのクマ事故で、命が失われたと記録されているのは、今回の3件のみ。
【制作メモ】ASSET-205 [Google Earth]
- 【Google Earth】
シーン: 山形県全体を俯瞰し、44年間で命が失われた記録が戸沢村の3件だけであることを地図の上で示す
検索座標: 38.7376, 140.1436（戸沢村役場）を画面上寄りに置き、山形県全体が入る構図
カメラ高度: 120,000〜150,000m
カメラ角度: 斜め30°、3D地形ON
向き: 南から北を見下ろす
地点A（戸沢村役場）: 38.7376, 140.1436
地点B（酒田市役所）: 38.9148, 139.8367
→ 編集者指示: 山形県の輪郭を白線でなぞる。戸沢村に赤ピンを1つだけ立て「1988年・3件」と表示し、県内の他の地点にはピンを立てない。「1977年〜2020年」のテロップを左上に。
